Details spectral_sqrt(B):

Good.

But if true A is the causal square root, Bf may not be in principal sqrt branch globally. We can correct branch by ensuring Af has support mostly first n: test a few sign choices? For complex, sign choices per frequency (Af or -Af) can be made to minimize energy outside 0..n-1. This is a global phase/discontinuity issue. Let's design approximate LP solver using subgradient/multiplicative weights. Since we just need a good g, not exact. We can solve dual approximately with **constraint selection**.

Dual: min ∑ y_k s.t. h_j = ∑_{r=0}^{n-1} f_r y_{j+r} ≥1, y≥0. This is covering. We can use a **greedy water-filling** algorithm:
- We want minimal mass. If f_r are weights centered at k, to satisfy h_j, choose y at positions k that maximize coverage per mass. Greedy: start with y=0, h=0. Find most violated j (h_j<1). Add mass Δ to position k that maximally increases h per cost? Since cost 1 per y, any k with largest f_{k-j}. But adding to k also helps others. Could add to argmax coverage. Repeat until h≥1. Then to reduce mass, remove from y where can without violating. This is like greedy set cover, may be okay.

But continuous mass and n large; O(n^2) maybe.

def _lp_refine(a0, end_time, seed):
  a=project_simplex(a0)*S? We'll choose S=1 for convenience? LP M then scale? Let's use S=1 for internal scores. Then M=target score/(2n). f sum=1. Uniform M=1/n. Current M ~1.5/(2n)=0.000197. Score vector returns 2n*max b.
  a sum=1.
  best=a, best_score=score.
  active init: top K of b=a*a plus mirror.
  for iter while time:
    solve reduced LP with active rows, M=current max_b (or target maybe best M? Use current f M).
    if success g sum>0:
      check full conv violations; if max violation >M*tol add top violators to active (up to Kmax)
      scale g to sum 1.
      try t in [1,0.7,0.4,0.2,0.1]: f_new=(1-t)a+t*g; score; pick best.
      if best update, reset no_improve; else reduce t/change active.
    if no progress, randomize active rows top current + random.
  return best
```

Detailed plan for final code:
1. Utilities: clean, project_simplex, normalize, score_vector (FFT), score from `evaluate_sequence` final.
2. Candidate generation:
   - Use `height_sequence_1` if available (clean, project to length n). This is current best; likely good.
   - Generate parametric shapes: power one-sided, symmetric two-tail, spectral flat root. More diverse.
   - Spectral flat root function: for target B:
      * Choose n. Build L=nextpow2(4n?) maybe. B normalized sum=1, max maybe target. Try several target shapes: flat, flat with edge taper, current/C^alpha.
      * Compute spectral root with principal sqrt and/or Newton from uniform.
   - Random smooth candidates: a_i ∝ exp(Σ Gaussian kernels) or exp(-((i-mu)^2/sigma^2)) sums.
   - Evaluate candidates, keep top K (e.g., 6).
3. Refinement algorithms:
   A. **LP row-generation refine** (`_lp_row_generation_refine`). This is new. Use current a start. For a given time, perform iterations. It needs solve reduced LP with scipy linprog. We need handle if SciPy unavailable.
   B. **Smooth surrogate L-BFGS refine** (improved from previous): maybe use on top starts after LP. Use log-sum-exp exact max smoothing with adaptive eps, L2/L3. But maybe LP enough. Keep some time for it.
   C. **Local random block / coordinate search** to final polish.

def _solve_reduced_lp(f, M, active, cap, timeout maybe):
    K=len(active); n=len(f)
    A=np.empty((K,n), dtype=np.float64) # fill zeros maybe
    A.fill? We can build column-wise? Dense row-wise:
    A=np.zeros((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for idx,k in enumerate(active):
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
        if hi>=lo:
            # r = k - j from k-lo down to k-hi
            A[idx, lo:hi+1] = f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
    c = np.full(n, -1.0)
    b = np.full(K, M)
    bounds=(0.0, cap)
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    if res.success: return res.x, K
    else if res.x finite: maybe use?

LP A build: Since f and M, active rows. Use scaled A = f/M. If M<=0, return inf.
```
def _build_lp_matrix(f, M, active):
    n=f.size; K=active.size
    A = np.zeros((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for idx,k in enumerate(active.tolist()):
       lo = max(0, k-n+1); hi = min(n-1, k)
       if hi >= lo:
           # indices j=lo..hi, r=k-j from k-lo down to k-hi
           counts = hi-lo+1
           A[idx, lo:hi+1] = f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1] / M
    return A
```
This loops K. Could optimize using vectorized but fine. For K=600, loop 600.

def _lp_solve(a, M, active, cap, use_bounds=True):
    if _linprog is None: return None
    n=a.size; K=active.size
    A = _build...
    c = np.full(n, -1.0)
    b = np.ones(K)
    res = _linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0, cap)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
    if res is None: return None
    x = np.asarray(res.x)
    if not finite: maybe use x from res? return None if None
    x = np.maximum(x,0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return None
    return x/s? Actually return x (unscaled) and success flag. We'll normalize outside.
```

       if it %? compute current b to update active if no or stale.

def _spectral_candidate(n, a0=None, alpha=0.5, max_iters=30):
    L = _pow2(4*n)
    a = _project_simplex(a0 if a0 is not None else np.full(n,1/n))
    A = np.zeros(L); A[:n]=a
    best=a; best_score=score
    for it in range(max_iters):
       Af = np.fft.rfft(A, n=L)?? If A already zero pad length L, no n? Use A padded length L. rfft(A) default length L? `np.fft.rfft(A)` length L//2+1. We'll maintain A length L.
       C = np.fft.irfft(Af*Af, n=L)[:2*n-1]
       C = np.maximum(C,0)
       sC=C.sum(); if sC<=0 break
       # construct B
       B = np.zeros(L)
       # Use C**alpha or min cap?
       B[:2*n-1] = np.power(C, alpha)
       sB=B.sum()
       if sB<=0 break
       B *= (sC/sB) # preserve sum =1? C sum=1 because a sum1; yes.
       Bf=np.fft.rfft(B)
       den=Af; scale=1e-12*max(1.0, np.max(np.abs(den)))
       den = den.copy(); den[np.abs(den)<scale] = scale
       newf = 0.5*(Af + Bf/den)
       newA = np.fft.irfft(newf, n=L)
       newA = np.real(newA)
       # maybe clean high? Set outside n to 0?
       cand = np.maximum(newA[:n], 0.0)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s<=0: break
       cand/=s
       sc=score(cand)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
           A = np.zeros(L); A[:n]=cand
           best=cand; best_score=sc
       else:
           # try blend with current
           for t in (0.5,0.25,0.1):
              blend=(1-t)*best + t*cand; normalize; if score improve update A
           break
    return best
```

def _random_smooth_sequence(n,seed,modalities=3):
  rng=default_rng(seed); centers=rng.uniform(0,n,modalities); sigmas=rng.uniform(n/20,n/6); amps=rng.uniform(0.5,2); base=np.zeros(n); for each: base+=amp*np.exp(-0.5*((j-c)/sigma)^2); base+=1e-3; return project(base)

```
def _spectral_step_once(a,L,alpha):
   Af=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
   C=np.fft.irfft(Af*Af,n=L)[:2*n-1]; C=np.maximum(C,0); s=C.sum()
   if s<=0: return None
   B=np.zeros(L); Bc=np.power(C, alpha); sB=Bc.sum(); if sB<=0 return None; B[:2*n-1]=Bc*(s/sB)
   Bf=np.fft.rfft(B)
   den=Af.copy(); scale=1e-12*max(1.0,float(np.max(np.abs(den))))
   den[np.abs(den)<scale]=scale
   newf=0.5*(Af+Bf/den)
   newA=np.fft.irfft(newf,n=L)
   return newA, C
```
Need if a length n.

def _cross_max(a,b,L):
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L); B=np.fft.rfft(b,n=L); c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,n=L)[:2*a.size-1]; return max, c
```

`_lp_solve_reduced(a,M,active,cap)`:
```
if _linprog is None or len(active)==0 or M<=0: return None
n=a.size; K=active.size
A=np.zeros((K,n),dtype=float)
invM=1.0/M
for idx,k in enumerate(active):
   lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
   if hi>=lo:
      A[idx, lo:hi+1] = (a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]) * invM
c=np.full(n,-1.0)
b=np.ones(K)
bounds=[(0.0,cap)]*n # maybe tuple
try:
  res=_linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
except Exception:
  return None
if res is None or res.x is None: return None
x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0); x=np.maximum(x,0.0); s=x.sum()
if s<=1e-10: return None
return x

def _lp_refine(a0,end_time,seed):
  if _linprog is None: return a0, score...
  a=project(a0); n; m; Lscore; best=...
  active=None; K0=250; Kmax=800; cap=10.0
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  no_improve=0; last_print=0; iter=0
  while time<end_time:
     if iter>=80: break
     # Ensure active set size grows
     if active is None or active.size < K0:
        c,_? = _conv_full(a,Lscore)
        Kcur=min(max(250, int(0.05*m?))? We'll set Kcur = min(Kmax, 300 + 20*iter)
        top = np.argsort(c)[-Kcur:][::-1]
        # include tail/random
        idx = top
        if idx.size < Kcur: pad random
        active=idx
     M = _score_vector(a,Lscore)/(2*n)
     # maybe recompute active each few iterations from current c
     if iter%3==0: recompute c and combine current active with top viol? We'll do below.
     g = _lp_solve_reduced(a,M,active,cap)
     if g is None: break
     # cross coefficients with a
     _, xc = _cross_coeffs(a,g,Lscore)
     viol = np.maximum(xc - M, 0.0)
     viol_max = float(np.max(viol)) if viol.size else 0.0
     if viol_max > M*1e-6:
        # add top violators
        cand_add = np.argsort(viol)[-100:][::-1]
        # also if active small, add top self peaks
        if active.size < 300:
           c = _conv_full(a,Lscore)
           cand_add = np.concatenate([cand_add, np.argsort(c)[-200:][::-1]])
        combined = np.concatenate([active, cand_add])
        combined = np.unique(combined)
        if combined.size > Kmax:
           vals = viol[combined] + 1e-6* _conv_full? maybe expensive; use viol + 0.001*M +? We need score. Use `rank = viol[combined] + 0.001*M`
           active = combined[np.argsort(rank)[-Kmax:][::-1]]
        else: active=combined
        iter +=1
        continue
     # feasible
     g_hat = g/g.sum()
     current_score = _score_vector(a,Lscore)
     best_trial=None; best_trial_score=current_score
     for t in (1.0, 0.7, 0.4, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05):
        cand = (1-t)*a + t*g_hat
        cand = _project_simplex(cand)
        sc=_score_vector(cand,Lscore)
        if sc < best_trial_score: best_trial_score=sc; best_trial=cand
     if best_trial is None: no_improve++ continue
     if best_trial_score < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_score=best_trial_score; best=best_trial; a=best_trial; no_improve=0; print
     else:
        no_improve +=1
        if best_trial_score < current_score -1e-12:
           a=best_trial
        # randomize active if no improve
        if no_improve %3==0:
           c=_conv_full(a,Lscore)
           top=np.argsort(c)[-max(200,K0):][::-1]
           active=np.unique(np.concatenate([top, np.floor(rng.uniform(0,m,size=100)).astype(int)]))[:Kmax]
     iter+=1
     if no_improve>10: break
  return best,best_score
```

def _score_vector(a, L=None):
  v=_clean_nonnegative(a); s=v.sum(); if s<=...; v=v/s;
  if L is None: L=pow2(2*n-1)
  A=np.fft.rfft(v,n=L); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1]; mx=max(c)
  return 2*n*mx
```

def _fallback_gradient_search(a0, end_time, seed):
  a=project; best; Lscore, Lgrad
  m=0,v=0; t=0; lr=1e-2; rng
  while time:
    c=conv; mx; q=2+3*frac
    w=np.power(c/mx, q-1); W=rfft(w,n=Lgrad); g=irfft(W*conj(rfft(a,n=Lgrad)))[:n]
    # subtract mean? To preserve sum, use a*(g-dot) in log? For simplex projected gradient, use g - mean maybe.
    g = a*(g - np.dot(a,g)) # tangent
    normalize g? Since a sum1, dot=0? Actually g from derivative not zero; dot may not zero. Use tangent and scale by norm.
    norm = sqrt(mean(g^2))
    step = lr*g/norm
    a_new=project(a - step)
    score check, adapt lr.
  return best
```

```
def _random_fourier_sequence(n, seed):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  k=np.arange(n)
  # random log spectrum decaying
  amp=np.exp(-np.arange(0,n//2)/ (n/10))
  phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,n//2+1)
  A=np.zeros(n//2+1); A[:]=amp*np.exp(1j*phase); A[0]=max(A[0],1.0)
  x=np.fft.irfft(A, n=n)
  x=np.abs(x)+1e-3
  return project(x)
```

Now, possible issue with **using A*A in DFT** vs **reversed**? DFT of linear convolution of sequences a and a is product DFT(a)*DFT(a). The coefficient at k=∑_{i=0}^k a_i a_{k-i}. That's np.convolve. Good. No reversal needed because np.convolve uses same orientation. For cross a*b, product. For gradient, we used conj(A) due reversal of weight? Let's rederive: c_k = ∑_i a_i a_{k-i}. derivative ∂c_k/∂a_j = ∑_{i: a_i?} Actually c_k = ∑_{i=0}^{k} a_i a_{k-i} with valid. Derivative wrt a_j: terms where i=j => a_{k-j}; terms where k-i=j => i=k-j => a_j? That term is a_{k-j} a_j, derivative a_{k-j}. Both if i≠k-i. So 2 a_{k-j} valid. For fixed j, sum over k: g_j = 2 ∑_k w_k a_{k-j}. This is 2 linear convolution? Define rev_a_l = a_{l}? Let r_{k-j}=a_{k-j}. We want sum over k w_k a_{k-j}. ```
def _local_search(a0,end_time,seed):
  a=_project_simplex(a0); best=a; best_score=score; Lscore=...
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  sigma=0.18; last_best=time.monotonic(); start=last_best
  while time<end_time:
    if time>end_time: break
    cand=best.copy()
    r=rng.random()
    if r < 0.55:
       # block
       maxw=max(3,n//8); w=int(np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(2), np.log(maxw))))
       s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
       cand[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,size=w))
    elif r<0.85:
       idx=int(rng.integers(0,n))
       cand[idx] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(sigma,0.5))))
       # maybe also neighbor
    else:
       # smooth low-freq perturb
       cand = best + rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.2, n)
    v=np.maximum(cand,0); s=v.sum(); if s>1e-15: v/=s else: v=_project_simplex(cand)
    sc=_score_vector(v,Lscore)
    if sc < best_score -1e-13: update sigma min(0.5, sigma*1.05)
    else: adapt sigma; if long no improve, random restart: v=best*exp(normal(0,0.03)); normalize; if improve update
  return best
```
Use `n` maybe 3809; computing maxw each iter. Good.

`_normalize` simpler:
```
def _normalize(v):
    v=_clean_nonnegative(v)
    s=float(v.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-15:
       n=v.size
       return np.full(n,1.0/n) if n else v
    return v/s
```

`_score_vector`:
```
def _score_vector(a, L=None):
    v=_normalize(a)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    if L is None: L=_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(v,n=L)
    C=A*A
    c=np.fft.irfft(C,n=L)[:2*n-1]
    if c.size==0: mx=0.0
    else:
       mx=float(np.max(c))
       if not np.isfinite(mx): return np.inf
       if mx<0: mx=0.0
    return 2.0*float(n)*mx
```

```

Now, spectral refinement details:
```
def _spectral_refine(a0,end_time,seed,alphas=(0.85,0.7,0.55,0.4,0.3,0.2)):
  a=_project_simplex(a0); n=a.size; best=a.copy(); best_score=_score_vector(a)
  if n<8: return
  Lscore=_pow2(2*n-1); Lroot=min(_pow2(4*n-3), 32768)
  A=np.zeros(Lroot); A[:n]=a
  last_best=time.monotonic(); start=last_best
  for alpha in alphas:
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time and no_improve < 12:
       # compute new
       A = np.zeros(Lroot); A[:n]=a
       Af=np.fft.rfft(A,n=Lroot)
       C=np.fft.irfft(Af*Af,n=Lroot)[:2*n-1]
       C=np.maximum(C,0); sC=C.sum()
       if sC<=0: break
       B=np.zeros(Lroot,dtype=float)
       Bc=np.power(C,float(alpha))
       sB=Bc.sum()
       if sB<=0: break
       B[:2*n-1] = Bc * (sC/sB)
       Bf=np.fft.rfft(B,n=Lroot)
       den=Af.astype(np.complex128).copy()
       scale=1e-12*max(1.0,float(np.max(np.abs(den))) if den.size else 1.0)
       den[np.abs(den)<scale]=scale
       newf=0.5*(Af + Bf/den)
       newA=np.fft.irfft(newf,n=Lroot)
       cand=np.maximum(np.real(newA[:n]),0.0)
       sc_sum=cand.sum()
       if sc_sum<=0: no_improve+=1; continue
       cand/=sc_sum
       sc=_score_vector(cand,Lscore)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
          best_score=sc; best=cand.copy(); a=cand.copy(); A[:n]=a; no_improve=0
       else:
          # blends
          improved=False
          for t in (0.5,0.25,0.1):
             blend=(1-t)*a + t*cand
             blend=_normalize(blend)
             sb=_score_vector(blend,Lscore)
             if sb < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score=sb; best=blend.copy(); a=blend.copy(); A[:n]=a; improved=True; break
          if not improved:
             no_improve +=1
             if no_improve>=2: break
    if time.monotonic() >= end_time: break
  return best,best_score
```
Need note `den[np.abs(den)<scale]=scale` sets complex to real scale (loses phase). Could use `den = np.where(np.abs(den) < scale, scale + 1j*0, den)`. If zero, arbitrary. Better:
```
bad=np.abs(den)<scale
den[bad]=scale * np.exp(1j*np.angle(den[bad]))
```
But angle for zero 0. Use that.

`np.angle` on zero okay 0. Good.

Maybe B should preserve sum not `sC/sB`? Since C sum=1 due a sum1, sC=1. Yes. If sC not due numerical, target sum should be sC. Good.

Alpha sequence: If no improve for one alpha, break to next smaller? In code no_improve >=2 break inner. Outer continues. Good. Time may still run.

Potential issue: If Lroot < 3n? For n=5000, Lroot=32768, 3n=15000, fine. For n cap 5000. Good.

Now L-BFGS. Let's write `_lbfgs_refine`:
```
def _lbfgs_refine(a0,end_time,seed):
  a=_project_simplex(a0); n=a.size; best=a.copy(); best_score=_score_vector(a)
  if _minimize is None or n<=1 or end_time-time.monotonic()<5: return best,best_score
  Lscore=_pow2(2*n-1); Lgrad=_pow2(3*n-2)
  x=_ratios_from_a(a)
  if x.size==0: return
  bounds=[(-30.0,30.0)]*x.size
  M0=best_score/(2*n)
  eps=max(1e-9, 0.25*M0)
  eps_min=max(1e-11, eps*1e-3)
  rng=default_rng(seed); no=0; last=time.monotonic()
  total=max(1e-6,end_time-last)
  # optional q2 stage 15%
  q_end=last+0.18*total
  while time<q_end:
     x,status=_run_minimize_once(_q_objective_grad,x,(2.0,),30,bounds)
     aa=_softmax_from_ratios(x); sc=_score_vector(aa)
     if sc<best_score-1e-12: update, status=0?; maybe no=0
     if no>2 break? Could just proceed.
  # q3 stage maybe 10%
  q3_end=last+0.30*total
  while time<q3_end:
     x,status=_run_minimize_once(_q_objective_grad,x,(3.0,),30,bounds)
     update
  # soft stage
  eps=max(eps_min, 0.12*best_score/(2*n))
  while time<end_time:
     x,status=_run_minimize_once(_soft_objective_grad,x,(eps,),40,bounds)
     aa=_softmax_from_ratios(x); sc=_score_vector(aa)
     if sc<best_score-1e-12:
        best=aa; best_score=sc; a=aa; eps=max(eps_min, eps*0.75); no=0
     else:
        no+=1; x=np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0,0.03,x.size), -30,30); eps=max(eps_min, eps*0.85)
        if no>=5 and eps <= eps_min*1.2: break
  return best,best_score
```
But after q2/q3, x may improve; update a. Need store `a`. For q stages, if no improve, still x changed? We can still keep x. Use `a = _softmax_from_ratios(x)` after each for score. If score improves update best but also set a maybe if finite. Good.

Now, final `propose_candidate` details:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget<=0: budget=1800.0
    deadline=t0+max(1.0,budget-30.0)
    seed=int(seed)
    # choose n
    n_target=4096
    ref_n, ref_arr = _get_reference()
    if ref_n is not None and 800 <= ref_n <= 5200:
        n_target=ref_n
    elif ref_n is not None and 128 <= ref_n:
        if ref_n < 5200: n_target=ref_n
    if n_target < 256: n_target=1024
    if n_target > 5200: n_target=5200
    # Short fallback
    if deadline <= t0+5:
       a=_power_sequence(n_target,0.5,1.0); out; validate; return
    # candidates
    cands=_generate_candidates(n_target, ref_arr if ref_n==n_target else None)
    scored=[]; Lscore=_pow2(2*n_target-1)
    for idx,a in enumerate(cands):
       sc=_score_vector(a,Lscore)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,idx,a))
    scored.sort()
    if not scored: a=_power_sequence... return
    best_a=np.asarray(scored[0][2]); best_score=scored[0][0]
    print...
    # quick spectral on top maybe 10% of budget
    if deadline - t0 > 30:
       end_s1=t0+0.10*(deadline-t0)
       a,sc=_spectral_refine(best_a,end_s1,seed+11)
       if sc<best_score: update
    # if second candidate distinct, quick spectral 5%
    if len(scored)>1 and deadline - t0 > 20:
       end_s2=t0+0.15*(deadline-t0)
       a,sc=_spectral_refine(scored[1][2],end_s2,seed+22)
       update
    # LP best for 50%
    end_lp=t0+0.62*(deadline-t0)
    a,sc=_lp_refine(best_a,end_lp,seed+33)
    update
    # LP second if available and time
    if len(scored)>1 and deadline - t0 > 15:
       end_lp2=t0+0.72*(deadline-t0)
       a,sc=_lp_refine(scored[1][2],end_lp2,seed+44)
       update
    # L-BFGS best 15%
    end_lb=t0+0.90*(deadline-t0)
    a,sc=_lbfgs_refine(best_a,end_lb,seed+55)
    update
    # final local 10%
    a,sc=_local_search(best_a,deadline,seed+66)
    update
    # final project and output
    best_a=_project_simplex(best_a)
    out=[float(x) for x in best_a]
    try: val=evaluate_sequence(out); if not finite: fallback
    except: pass
    print final score
    return out
```

def _generate_candidates(n, ref_arr=None):

    if r.size==n: cands.append(r)

  cands.append(_power_sequence(n,0.45,1.0))
  cands.append(_power_sequence(n,0.7,1.0))

def _edge_sequence(n, p=0.5, boost=0.5, gamma=2.0):
  j=np.arange(n); base=np.power(j+0.5,-p)+np.power(n-1-j+0.5,-p)
  t=j/(n-1); base *= 1 + boost*np.power(1.0 - np.sin(np.pi*t), gamma)

def _lowfreq_sequence(n, seed, rmax=4):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  i=np.arange(n,dtype=float); theta=2*np.pi*i/n
  x=np.full(n,1.0)
  for r in range(1,rmax+1):
     w=float(rng.integers(1, max(2, rmax+1))) # use 1..rmax? 
     amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.8))*np.exp(-w/4.0)
     ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
     w_freq = float(rng.integers(1, rmax+1))
     x += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*w_freq*i/n + ph)
  x=np.maximum(x,1e-8)
  return _project_simplex(x)
```

def _lp_refine(a0,end_time,seed):
    if _linprog is None:
       return a0, np.inf? no score
    a=_project_simplex(a0)
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1; Lscore=_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_score_vector(a,Lscore)
    active=None
    Kmax = 1000 if n>2500 else 1200? Let's compute: if n=3809, 1000; if n=1000, 1500.
    Kmax = int(min(1200, max(300, 8000000//n)))? 8M elements cap. For n=3809, 2098? Wait 8M/n=2100. Let's set Kmax=min(1200, max(300, 5000000//n)). For n=3809, 1312 ->1200. For n=5000, 1000. For n=1000, 1200. Good.
    K0 = max(150, Kmax//4)
    cap=10.0
    no_improve=0; iter=0; last_print=t0
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    while time<end_time:
        if iter >= 60: break
        if active is None:
           c=_conv_full(a,Lscore)
           Kcur=min(Kmax, K0)
           top=np.argsort(c)[-Kcur:][::-1]
           tail=np.array([0,1,2,3,m-4,m-3,m-2,m-1],dtype=int) if m>8 else np.array([0])
           active=np.unique(np.concatenate([top,tail]))[:Kmax]
        score_a=_score_vector(a,Lscore)
        M=score_a/(2*n)
        if M<=0: break
        # If active size small or periodic, refresh to keep top current peaks
        if active.size < K0 or iter % 5 == 0:
            c=_conv_full(a,Lscore)
            top=np.argsort(c)[-min(Kmax, max(K0, active.size+50)):][::-1]
            keep = active[:max(100, active.size//2)]
            cand = np.concatenate([keep, top])
            cand = np.unique(cand)[:Kmax]
            active=cand
        g=_lp_solve_reduced(a,M,active,cap)
        if g is None:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 5: break
            # randomize active
            c=_conv_full(a,Lscore)
            active = np.argsort(c)[-K0:][::-1]
            continue
        _, xc = _cross_coeffs(a,g,Lscore)
        viol=xc-M; np.maximum(viol,0,out=viol)
        vm=float(np.max(viol)) if viol.size else 0.0
        if vm > max(1e-8, 0.05*M):
            # add violations
            add=np.argsort(viol)[-min(200, m):][::-1]
            keep=active[:max(100, active.size//2)]
            combined=np.unique(np.concatenate([keep, add]))
            if combined.size > Kmax:
                rank=viol[combined] + 1e-6*M
                combined=combined[np.argsort(rank)[-Kmax:][::-1]]
            active=combined
            iter +=1
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve>8: break
            continue
        # feasible-ish
        gs=float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-12:
            no_improve+=1; continue
        g_hat=g/gs
        best_trial=None; best_trial_score=score_a
        for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.12, 0.05):
            cand=(1-t)*a + t*g_hat
            cand=_project_simplex(cand) # or normalize
            sc=_score_vector(cand,Lscore)
            if sc < best_trial_score:
                best_trial_score=sc; best_trial=cand.copy()
        if best_trial is not None and best_trial_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=best_trial_score; best=best_trial; a=best_trial; no_improve=0
            print(...)
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if best_trial is not None and best_trial_score < score_a -1e-12:
                a=best_trial  # move current but not global
            # random active occasionally
            if no_improve % 3 ==0:
                c=_conv_full(a,Lscore)
                top=np.argsort(c)[-K0:][::-1]
                rand=np.floor(rng.uniform(0,m,size=min(100,m))).astype(int)
                active=np.unique(np.concatenate([top, rand]))[:Kmax]
        iter += 1
        if no_improve > 12: break
        # time gap print every 30s
    return best,best_score
```

def _gradient_search(a0,end_time,seed):
   a=project; best; Lscore; Lgrad
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=1e-2; rng
   while time:
      c=conv; mx; if mx<=0 break; f=mx
      w=np.power(c/mx, 2.0) # peak focus
      W=np.fft.rfft(w[:...], n=Lgrad); A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=Lgrad)
      ga=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A), n=Lgrad)[:n]
      dot=np.dot(a,ga); g=a*(ga-dot)
      # normalize g
      rms=np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g)); if rms>0: g/=rms
      # Adam
      ...
      cand=normalize(a-lr*step)
      sc=score; update, adapt lr
```

def _lowfreq_sequence(n, seed, rmax=4):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(rmax):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, rmax + 1))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.9)) * np.exp(-freq / 4.0)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586))
        x += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t / float(n) + ph)
    x = np.maximum(x, 1e-8)
    return _project_simplex(x)
```

def _edge_boost_sequence(n, p=0.5, offset=1.0, boost=0.5, gamma=2.0):
  j=np.arange(n); base=np.power(j+offset,-p)+np.power(n-1-j+offset,-p)
  t=j/ max(1,n-1); boost_factor=1 + boost*np.power(1.0 - np.sin(np.pi*t), gamma)

```

def _cross_coeffs(a,b,L=None):
    va=_clean_nonnegative(a)
    sa=va.sum(); if sa>0: va=va/sa
    vb=_clean_nonnegative(b)
    n=va.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if L is None: L=_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(va,n=L); B=np.fft.rfft(vb,n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,n=L)[:2*n-1]
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function height sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)**2.

The code combines several search ideas:
  * Strong non-uniform parametric initial shapes.
  * A spectral square-root/flattening iteration based on solving A^2 ~= B
    in the Fourier algebra, accepting only genuine improvements.
  * A cutting-plane / active-set LP heuristic inspired by the previous
    state-of-the-art: for the current f, maximize sum(g) subject to
    approximately (f*g) <= ||f*f||_inf, using a generated set of active
    rows and dense HiGHS solves when available.
  * Smooth L-BFGS-B polishing on L2/L3/log-sum-exp surrogates.
  * Final stochastic multiplicative block local search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
except Exception:
    _minimize = None
    _linprog = None


def _pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_nonnegative(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(v, 0.0)


def _normalize(v):
    v = _clean_nonnegative(v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return v / s


def _project_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = 1}."""
    v = _clean_nonnegative(v)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    if float(v.sum()) <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(x)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = x > (css - 1.0) / idx

    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    r = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    tau = (css[r] - 1.0) / float(r + 1)
    out = v - tau
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)

    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return out / s


def _get_reference_sequence():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is None:
        return None, None
    arr = np.asarray(h)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        return None, None
    arr = _clean_nonnegative(arr)
    if arr.size < 32:
        return None, None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None, None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None, None
    return int(arr.size), arr / s


def _power_sequence(n, p=0.5, offset=1.0):
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.maximum(j + float(offset), 1e-9)
    return _project_simplex(np.power(x, -float(p)))


def _sym_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, offset=1.0):
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = np.power(np.maximum(j + float(offset), 1e-9), -float(p))
    right = np.power(np.maximum((float(n - 1) - j) + float(offset), 1e-9), -float(p))
    return _project_simplex(left + right)


def _edge_boost_sequence(n, p=0.5, offset=1.0, boost=0.75, gamma=2.0):
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = _sym_power_sequence(n, p, offset)
    t = j / float(max(1, n - 1))
    edge = 1.0 - np.sin(np.pi * t)
    weights = base * (1.0 + float(boost) * np.power(edge, float(gamma)))
    return _project_simplex(weights)


def _lowfreq_sequence(n, seed, rmax=4):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(rmax):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, rmax + 1))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.9)) * np.exp(-freq / 4.0)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586))
        x += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t / float(n) + ph)
    x = np.maximum(x, 1e-8)
    return _project_simplex(x)


def _generate_candidates(n, ref_arr=None):
    cands = []

    if ref_arr is not None:
        ref = _normalize(ref_arr)
        if ref.size == n and float(ref.sum()) > 0.0:
            cands.append(ref.copy())
            cands.append(_project_simplex(ref + ref[::-1]))

    cands.append(_power_sequence(n, 0.45, 0.75))
    cands.append(_power_sequence(n, 0.50, 1.00))
    cands.append(_power_sequence(n, 0.55, 1.25))
    cands.append(_power_sequence(n, 0.70, 1.50))

    cands.append(_sym_power_sequence(n, 0.35, 1.0))
    cands.append(_sym_power_sequence(n, 0.45, 1.0))
    cands.append(_sym_power_sequence(n, 0.55, 1.0))
    cands.append(_sym_power_sequence(n, 0.70, 1.0))

    cands.append(_edge_boost_sequence(n, 0.45, 1.0, 0.45, 2.0))
    cands.append(_edge_boost_sequence(n, 0.50, 1.0, 0.90, 2.0))
    cands.append(_edge_boost_sequence(n, 0.60, 1.0, 1.40, 3.0))

    for seed in (11, 23, 37, 53, 71, 97, 127):
        cands.append(_lowfreq_sequence(n, seed, 3))
        cands.append(_lowfreq_sequence(n, seed + 1000, 5))

    return cands


def _score_vector(a, L=None):
    """Exact normalized objective: 2*n*max(conv(a,a))."""
    v = _normalize(a)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    if L is None:
        L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)

    A = np.fft.rfft(v, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf

    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx


def _conv_full(a, L=None):
    """Full linear convolution coefficients of normalized a with itself."""
    v = _normalize(a)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(v, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _cross_coeffs(a, b, L=None):
    """Linear convolution of normalized a with nonnegative b (b not normalized)."""
    va = _clean_nonnegative(a)
    sa = float(va.sum())
    if sa > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sa):
        va = va / sa
    vb = _clean_nonnegative(b)
    n = va.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)

    A = np.fft.rfft(va, n=L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(vb, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _spectral_refine(a0, end_time, seed, alphas=(0.90, 0.75, 0.60, 0.45, 0.35, 0.25)):
    """
    Spectral fixed-point iteration:
      C = a*a, B = normalize(C**alpha), A_new ~= sqrt(B) via Newton in Fourier.
    Only updates are accepted if the exact score improves.
    """
    a = _project_simplex(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 8:
        return a, _score_vector(a)

    Lscore = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lroot = min(_pow2(4 * n - 3), 32768)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_vector(a, Lscore)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, end_time - start)
    if total < 5.0:
        return best, best_score

    # Split time among alpha levels, with more time on flatter targets.
    folds = (0.18, 0.18, 0.18, 0.16, 0.14, 0.16)
    used_frac = 0.0
    for idx, alpha in enumerate(alphas):
        if time.monotonic() >= end_time:
            break
        fr = folds[idx] if idx < len(folds) else 0.1
        stage_end = min(end_time, start + (used_frac + fr) * total)
        used_frac += fr
        if stage_end <= time.monotonic() + 0.5:
            continue

        no_improve = 0
        while time.monotonic() < stage_end and no_improve < 12:
            A = np.zeros(Lroot, dtype=np.float64)
            A[:n] = a

            Af = np.fft.rfft(A, n=Lroot)
            C = np.fft.irfft(Af * Af, n=Lroot)[:2 * n - 1]
            np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
            sC = float(C.sum())
            if sC <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(sC):
                break

            B = np.zeros(Lroot, dtype=np.float64)
            Bc = np.power(C, float(alpha))
            sB = float(Bc.sum())
            if sB <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(sB):
                break
            B[:2 * n - 1] = Bc * (sC / sB)

            Bf = np.fft.rfft(B, n=Lroot)
            den = Af.astype(np.complex128, copy=True)
            scale = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(den))) if den.size else 1.0)
            bad = np.abs(den) < scale
            if np.any(bad):
                ang = np.angle(den[bad])
                den[bad] = scale * np.exp(1j * ang)

            newf = 0.5 * (Af + Bf / den)
            newA = np.fft.irfft(newf, n=Lroot)

            cand = np.maximum(np.real(newA[:n]), 0.0)
            sc = float(cand.sum())
            if sc <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(sc):
                no_improve += 1
                continue
            cand /= sc
            cand = _project_simplex(cand)
            scv = _score_vector(cand, Lscore)

            if np.isfinite(scv) and scv < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = scv
                best = cand.copy()
                a = cand.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                improved_blend = False
                for mix in (0.55, 0.35, 0.18, 0.08):
                    blend = _project_simplex((1.0 - mix) * a + mix * cand)
                    sb = _score_vector(blend, Lscore)
                    if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_score = sb
                        best = blend.copy()
                        a = blend.copy()
                        improved_blend = True
                        no_improve = 0
                        break
                if not improved_blend:
                    no_improve += 1
                    xjitter = 1.0
                    if no_improve >= 2:
                        break

        # Occasional tiny spectral restart around best to escape very flat stages.
        if time.monotonic() < stage_end and best_score > 1.50:
            jittered = _normalize(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)))
            sj = _score_vector(jittered, Lscore)
            if np.isfinite(sj) and sj < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sj
                best = jittered.copy()
                a = jittered.copy()

    return best, best_score


def _lp_solve_reduced(a, M, active, cap):
    """
    Solve reduced primal LP:
      maximize sum(g)
      s.t. (f/M * g)[k] <= 1 for active rows k
           0 <= g <= cap.
    """
    if _linprog is None:
        return None
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    K = int(active.size)
    if n <= 0 or K <= 0:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    invM = 1.0 / M

    for idx, k in enumerate(active):
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            A[idx, lo:hi + 1] = (a[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]) * invM

    c = np.full(n, -1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, float(cap))] * n

    try:
        res = _linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return x


def _lp_refine(a0, end_time, seed):
    """
    Active-set LP refinement:
      Iterate f <- convex combination of f and a normalized g solving
      max sum(g) subject to (f*g) approximately bounded by current peak.
    """
    if _linprog is None:
        return a0, np.inf

    a = _project_simplex(a0)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if n < 16 or m < 16:
        return a, _score_vector(a)

    Lscore = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_vector(a, Lscore)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    Kmax = int(min(1200, max(350, 5000000 // max(1, n))))
    K0 = int(max(200, Kmax // 4))
    cap = 10.0

    active = None
    iter_no = 0
    no_improve = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        if iter_no >= 70:
            break

        # Initial / refresh active set from current convolution peaks.
        if active is None or active.size < K0 or iter_no % 6 == 0:
            c = _conv_full(a, Lscore)
            topK = min(Kmax, max(K0, int(active.size * 0.5) + 80 if active is not None else K0))
            top = np.argsort(c)[-topK:][::-1]
            if active is None:
                tail = np.array([0, 1, 2, 3, m - 4, m - 3, m - 2, m - 1], dtype=np.int64)
                active = np.unique(np.concatenate([top, tail]))[:Kmax]
            else:
                keep = active[:max(120, int(active.size * 0.5))]
                active = np.unique(np.concatenate([keep, top]))[:Kmax]

        score_a = _score_vector(a, Lscore)
        if not np.isfinite(score_a):
            break
        M = score_a / (2.0 * float(n))
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        g = _lp_solve_reduced(a, M, active, cap)
        if g is None:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 6:
                break
            c = _conv_full(a, Lscore)
            active = np.unique(np.argsort(c)[-K0:][::-1])[:Kmax]
            iter_no += 1
            continue

        # Check full feasibility for the original M.
        xc = _cross_coeffs(a, g, Lscore)
        viol = xc - M
        np.maximum(viol, 0.0, out=viol)
        viol_max = float(np.max(viol)) if viol.size else 0.0

        # If many unmodeled rows are violated, add the worst offenders.
        if viol_max > max(1e-9, 0.05 * M):
            addK = min(220, m)
            add = np.argsort(viol)[-addK:][::-1]
            keep = active[:max(120, int(active.size * 0.55))]
            combined = np.unique(np.concatenate([keep, add]))
            if combined.size > Kmax:
                rank = viol[combined] + 1e-6 * M
                combined = combined[np.argsort(rank)[-Kmax:][::-1]]
            active = combined
            no_improve += 1
            iter_no += 1
            if no_improve > 9:
                break
            continue

        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-12:
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        g_hat = g / gs

        best_trial = None
        best_trial_score = score_a
        for t in (1.0, 0.85, 0.65, 0.45, 0.28, 0.15, 0.07):
            cand = _project_simplex((1.0 - float(t)) * a + float(t) * g_hat)
            sc = _score_vector(cand, Lscore)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score:
                best_trial_score = sc
                best_trial = cand.copy()

        if best_trial is not None and best_trial_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = best_trial_score
            best = best_trial
            a = best_trial
            no_improve = 0
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now - last_print > 8.0:
                print(f"  lp iter {iter_no:3d} score {best_score:.8f}")
                last_print = now
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if best_trial is not None and best_trial_score < score_a - 1e-12:
                a = best_trial

            # If stuck, randomize some active rows around current peaks.
            if no_improve % 3 == 0:
                c = _conv_full(a, Lscore)
                top = np.argsort(c)[-K0:][::-1]
                rand = np.floor(rng.uniform(0.0, float(m), size=min(120, m))).astype(np.int64)
                active = np.unique(np.concatenate([top, rand]))[:Kmax]

        iter_no += 1
        if no_improve > 12:
            break

    return best, best_score


def _ratios_from_a(a):
    """
    Positive simplex parameterization by log-ratios to the last coordinate.
    Adds a tiny floor to avoid zeros.
    """
    a = _clean_nonnegative(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    if float(a.sum()) <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(a.sum()):
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / float(a.sum())

    if n == 1:
        return a

    # Floor so the reference entry is never too tiny.
    a = a + 1e-8
    a = a / float(a.sum())

    ref = float(a[-1])
    if ref < 1e-14:
        a[-1] = 1e-8
        a = a / float(a.sum())
        ref = float(a[-1])

    x = np.log(np.maximum(a[:-1], 1e-300) / max(ref, 1e-300))
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    return np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)


def _softmax_from_ratios(x):
    """Reconstruct positive softmax vector from log(a_i / a_last)."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    n = x.size + 1
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    m = 0.0
    if x.size:
        mm = float(np.max(x))
        if mm > m:
            m = mm

    e = np.exp(np.clip(x - m, -745.0, 709.0))
    e_last = float(np.exp(-m))
    s = float(e.sum()) + e_last

    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out[:-1] = e / s
    out[-1] = e_last / s
    return out


def _q_objective_grad(x, q=2.0, scale=1.0):
    """
    Objective Q_q(a) = scale * sum_k (a*a)_k^q,
    gradient with respect to log-ratio variables.
    """
    a = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    L = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)

    qq = float(q)
    w = c
    if qq != 2.0:
        w = np.power(c, qq - 1.0)

    f = float(np.sum(np.power(c, qq))) * float(scale)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L)
    ga = (2.0 * qq) * np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), n=L)[:n]

    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gfull = a * (ga - dot)
    gy = gfull[:-1] * float(scale)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)):
        gy = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e300
    return f, gy


def _soft_objective_grad(x, eps=1e-5, scale=1.0):
    """
    Smoothed max objective:
      scale * (cmax + eps * logsumexp((c-cmax)/eps)), c = a*a.
    Gradient wrt log-ratio variables.
    """
    a = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    L = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    if eps <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(eps):
        eps = 1e-12
    z = (c - cmax) / eps
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    sume = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sume) or sume <= 0.0:
        return 1e300, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    f = (cmax + eps * float(np.log(sume))) * float(scale)
    w = e / sume

    W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L)
    ga = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), n=L)[:n]

    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gfull = a * (ga - dot)
    gy = gfull[:-1] * float(scale)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)):
        gy = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e300
    return f, gy


def _run_minimize_once(fun, x, args, maxiter, bounds=None):
    if _minimize is None:
        return np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), 99
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x, 1
    if bounds is None:
        bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * int(x.size)

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "disp": 0,
        "maxfun": int(max(120, 9 * int(maxiter))),
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
    }
    try:
        res = _minimize(
            fun,
            x,
            args=args,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
    except Exception:
        return x, 99

    x_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.where(np.isfinite(x_new), x_new, 0.0)
    x_new = np.clip(x_new, -40.0, 40.0)
    return x_new, 0


def _gradient_search(a0, end_time, seed):
    """
    Simple adaptive subgradient / Adam search used if L-BFGS is unavailable,
    and also useful as a fallback.
    """
    a = _project_simplex(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 8 or _minimize is not None:
        return a, _score_vector(a)

    Lscore = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lgrad = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_vector(best, Lscore)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    tstep = 0
    lr = 1e-2
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    last_best = time.monotonic()
    start = last_best
    total = max(1e-6, end_time - start)

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        elapsed = max(0.0, time.monotonic() - start)
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / total)
        c = _conv_full(a, Lscore)
        mx = float(c.max())
        if mx <= 1e-300:
            break

        score = _score_vector(a, Lscore)
        now = time.monotonic()
        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            last_best = now
            lr = min(3e-2, lr * 1.05)

        if now - last_best > 8.0:
            lr = max(5e-5, lr * 0.70)
            if now - last_best > 25.0:
                a = _project_simplex(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, size=n)))
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                tstep = 0

        q = 2.0 + 3.0 * frac
        w = np.power(c / mx, q - 1.0)
        w = 0.6 * w + 0.4 * (c / mx)

        W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=Lgrad)
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=Lgrad)
        ga = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), n=Lgrad)[:n]
        dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
        g = a * (ga - dot)

        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g))) + 1e-12
        g = g / rms

        tstep += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)
        mh = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** tstep)
        vh = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** tstep)
        step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)

        a_new = _project_simplex(a - step)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)):
            a = best.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            tstep = 0
        else:
            a = a_new

    return best, best_score


def _lbfgs_refine(a0, end_time, seed):
    """
    Polish with smooth surrogates: L2, L3, then annealed smoothed max.
    """
    a = _project_simplex(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a, _score_vector(a)

    Lscore = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_vector(best, Lscore)

    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1e-6, end_time - start)
    if total < 6.0:
        return best, best_score

    if _minimize is None:
        # Use the lightweight gradient search for the whole budget.
        return _gradient_search(a, end_time, seed)

    x = _ratios_from_a(best)
    best_x = x.copy()
    bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * int(x.size)
    if x.size == 0:
        return best, best_score

    M0 = best_score / (2.0 * float(n))
    scale = 1.0 / max(M0, 1e-12)

    # Stage 1: L2 surrogate.
    q2_end = start + 0.16 * total
    while time.monotonic() < q2_end:
        x, _ = _run_minimize_once(_q_objective_grad, x, (2.0, scale), 35, bounds)
        aa = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
        sc = _score_vector(aa, Lscore)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc
            best = aa.copy()
            best_x = x.copy()
        elif not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            x = best_x.copy()

    # Stage 2: L3 surrogate.
    q3_end = start + 0.32 * total
    if time.monotonic() < q3_end:
        while time.monotonic() < q3_end:
            x, _ = _run_minimize_once(_q_objective_grad, x, (3.0, scale), 35, bounds)
            aa = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
            sc = _score_vector(aa, Lscore)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc
                best = aa.copy()
                best_x = x.copy()
            elif not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                x = best_x.copy()

    # Stage 3: annealed smoothed max.
    eps = max(1e-9, 0.18 * best_score / (2.0 * float(n)))
    eps_min = max(1e-10, eps * 1e-3)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve = 0
    x = best_x.copy()

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        x, _ = _run_minimize_once(_soft_objective_grad, x, (eps, scale), 45, bounds)
        aa = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
        sc = _score_vector(aa, Lscore)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc
            best = aa.copy()
            best_x = x.copy()
            eps = max(eps_min, eps * 0.78)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            eps = max(eps_min, eps * 0.88)
            if no_improve >= 5:
                x = best_x.copy()
                x = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.025, size=x.size), -40.0, 40.0)
            elif no_improve >= 2:
                x = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.015, size=x.size), -40.0, 40.0)
            elif not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                x = best_x.copy()

            if no_improve >= 9 and eps <= eps_min * 1.15:
                break

    aa = _softmax_from_ratios(x)
    sc = _score_vector(aa, Lscore)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
        best_score = sc
        best = aa.copy()

    return best, best_score


def _local_search(a0, end_time, seed):
    """
    Final stochastic multiplicative local search.
    Accepts only exact objective improvements.
    """
    a = _project_simplex(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 8:
        return a, _score_vector(a)

    Lscore = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_vector(best, Lscore)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    sigma = 0.16
    last_best = time.monotonic()
    start = last_best

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        cand = best.copy()
        r = rng.random()

        if r < 0.60:
            maxw = max(3, n // 7)
            w = int(np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(2.0), np.log(float(maxw)))))
            w = max(1, min(w, n))
            s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
            cand[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w))
        elif r < 0.85:
            idx = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            cand[idx] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(sigma, 0.45))))
            if idx + 1 < n:
                cand[idx + 1] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(sigma, 0.45))))
            if idx - 1 >= 0:
                cand[idx - 1] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(sigma, 0.45))))
        else:
            # Low-frequency perturbation.
            th = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
            amp = sigma * 0.35
            c1 = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
            c2 = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
            shift = amp * (c1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * th / float(n)) +
                           c2 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * th / float(n)))
            cand = best + shift

        cand = _normalize(cand)
        sc = _score_vector(cand, Lscore)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc
            best = cand.copy()
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            sigma = min(0.45, sigma * 1.06)
        else:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now - last_best > 4.0:
                sigma = max(0.025, sigma * 0.78)
                if now - last_best > 22.0 and now - start > 50.0:
                    jitter = _normalize(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.035, size=n)))
                    js = _score_vector(jitter, Lscore)
                    if np.isfinite(js) and js < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_score = js
                        best = jitter.copy()
                        last_best = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Return the best nonnegative sequence found before the time budget.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 10.0:
        budget = 1800.0

    # Leave a small margin for final FFT / official evaluation.
    safety = 30.0
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - safety)

    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42

    ref_n, ref_arr = _get_reference_sequence()

    # Prefer the provided construction length, but keep the problem tractable.
    n_target = 4096
    if ref_n is not None and 800 <= ref_n <= 5200:
        n_target = int(ref_n)
    elif ref_n is not None and 128 <= ref_n <= 5200:
        n_target = int(ref_n)
    if n_target < 256:
        n_target = 1024
    if n_target > 5200:
        n_target = 5200

    # Very short fallback.
    if deadline <= t0 + 8.0:
        a = _power_sequence(n_target, 0.5, 1.0)
        return [float(x) for x in a]

    Lscore = _pow2(2 * n_target - 1)

    # Initial candidate generation and cheap ranking.
    cands = _generate_candidates(n_target, ref_arr)
    scored = []
    for i, cand in enumerate(cands):
        sc = _score_vector(cand, Lscore)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), int(i), cand.copy()))
    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    if not scored:
        a = _power_sequence(n_target, 0.5, 1.0)
        return [float(x) for x in a]

    best_a = np.asarray(scored[0][2], dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_score = float(scored[0][0])
    print(f"initial best score: {best_score:.8f}, n={n_target}, cands={len(scored)}")

    total_budget = max(1.0, deadline - t0)

    def _stage_end(frac_of_full):
        return t0 + frac_of_full * total_budget

    # 1) Quick spectral flattening from best and second starts.
    if deadline - t0 > 30.0:
        a, sc = _spectral_refine(best_a, _stage_end(0.10), seed + 17)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"  spectral 1: {best_score:.8f}")

    if len(scored) > 1 and deadline - t0 > 25.0:
        a, sc = _spectral_refine(scored[1][2], _stage_end(0.16), seed + 29)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"  spectral 2: {best_score:.8f}")

    # 2) Main active-set LP refinement on the current best.
    if _linprog is not None and deadline - t0 > 60.0:
        a, sc = _lp_refine(best_a, _stage_end(0.62), seed + 311)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"  lp main: {best_score:.8f}")

    # 3) LP refinement from the second initial basin if it remains promising.
    if _linprog is not None and len(scored) > 1 and deadline - t0 > 45.0:
        a, sc = _lp_refine(scored[1][2], _stage_end(0.72), seed + 397)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"  lp second: {best_score:.8f}")

    # 4) L-BFGS / surrogate polish.
    if deadline - t0 > 60.0:
        a, sc = _lbfgs_refine(best_a, _stage_end(0.90), seed + 523)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"  lbfgs polish: {best_score:.8f}")

    # 5) Final stochastic local search.
    if deadline - t0 > 25.0:
        a, sc = _local_search(best_a, deadline, seed + 641)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"  local final: {best_score:.8f}")

    best_a = _project_simplex(best_a)
    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    # Final sanity check against the official evaluator if available.
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(out)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            out = [float(x) for x in _power_sequence(len(out), 0.5, 1.0)]
        else:
            best_score = float(min(best_score, val))
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"final internal/official score: {best_score:.8f}")
    return out
```
